**Latest formalization-and-family-security rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [ILO South Asia formalization and child-labour record](records/comparative-ilo-south-asia-formalization-child-labour-voice-2026.json) adds a regional implementation layer connecting informal work, supply-chain risk, worker voice, social protection, community action, and child-labour prevention. It reports project reach and policy measures, not causal or post-project durability evidence; the active next test follows workers and families through registration, wages, benefits, schooling, complaints, and local enforcement after project closure.

**Latest labour-governance institution rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [ILO Kurdistan tripartite social-dialogue record](records/comparative-ilo-kurdistan-tripartite-social-dialogue-labour-governance-2026.json) adds the newly established Forum as a standing voice institution with equal government, worker, and employer representation and an implementation-follow-up mandate. Its durability depends on member ownership; the next test is an issue-level trace from deliberation through rule, enforcement, remedy, and worker or firm outcome.

**Latest consumer-confidence asymmetry rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [Ipsos September global consumer-confidence record](records/comparative-ipsos-global-consumer-confidence-september-2026-subindex-asymmetry.json) adds a cross-country multi-clock consumer route: current purchasing and investment confidence remain materially below future expectations and job sentiment while the aggregate index is stable. It is a perception index, not realized household behavior; the next test follows country or group-level sentiment into spending, saving, debt, employment, and political attribution.

**Latest health-capacity rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [WHO essential-public-health-functions record](records/comparative-who-essential-public-health-functions-institutional-capacity-2026.json) adds a maintenance-and-financing layer: more than 120 countries identify essential public-health institutional capacity as a medium-to-high priority, while WHO describes curative budget skew, fragmented programmes, uneven workforce investment, and donor-dependent prevention. The next test follows one country through financing, routine capacity, household burden, crisis response, and public trust; no comparable country capacity or outcome claim is made.

**Latest prosperity-distribution rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [World Bank Uzbekistan poverty and equity record](records/comparative-world-bank-uzbekistan-poverty-middle-class-job-quality-2026.json) adds a distributional contrast: rapid poverty and middle-class improvement can coexist with informal low-productivity job creation, Tashkent-centered opportunity, care-constrained women’s employment, weak learning, and reduced cash-transfer coverage. The next test is longitudinal household mobility and fallback capacity, not a causal claim from the assessment.

**Latest rural-urban-transition rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [World Bank Laos poverty and urban-vulnerability record](records/comparative-world-bank-laos-poverty-urban-vulnerability-remittances-2026.json) adds a rural-led poverty-reduction pathway that does not automatically yield urban security: poverty fell nationally while urban poverty, informal work, migration dependence, youth inactivity, and ethnic disparities remain active constraints. The next test follows a group or migration corridor through jobs, remittances, housing, schooling, care, and return-to-poverty risk.

**Latest regional-AI-governance rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [ILO ASEAN-China AI labour-policy record](records/comparative-ilo-asean-china-ai-labour-policy-dialogue-2026.json) adds a South-South governance layer in which governments, workers, and employers negotiate employment-friendly AI, reskilling, public employment services, digital access, and standards coordination. It reports policy dialogue, not implemented governance or worker outcomes; the next test is an issue-level path from regional agenda to national rule, firm practice, worker voice, and remedy.

Latest completed rotation: Gallup classroom-device governance. Added a machine-readable record and cross-domain memo testing whether frontline teacher oversight mediates technology legitimacy; retained explicit cross-sectional and non-causal boundaries.

**Latest comparative consumer-legitimacy rotation — 2026-09-29:** the [Ipsos Thailand September 2026 record](records/comparative-ipsos-thailand-consumer-sentiment-september-2026-corruption-safety-spending.json) adds a route from corruption, public safety, unemployment, inequality and national-direction judgments through resilient routine purchasing but weaker major-purchase and economic confidence. Its next test is whether concern and spending splits map to household budgets, debt, subsidy receipt, crime exposure, labor-market trajectories and political attribution; source-defined aggregate perceptions remain insufficient realized-outcome evidence.

**Latest care-and-energy rotation — 2026-09-28:** the [Small Acts of Kindness/Ipsos older-people record](records/comparative-ipsos-small-acts-kindness-older-people-winter-energy-loneliness-2026.json) adds a route from energy and food trade-offs through isolation, mediated companionship, printed information, material warmth and perceived care in a targeted support encounter. Its next test is whether support changes energy use, food adequacy, benefits or advice take-up, health, service continuity and repeat connection; selected recipient feedback remains insufficient outcome or population evidence.

**Latest cultural-institution rotation — 2026-09-28:** the [Ipsos–Independent Football Regulator club-heritage record](records/comparative-ipsos-independent-football-regulator-club-heritage-fan-voice-2026.json) adds a route from club history, place, ritual, community presence and fan engagement through distress, prospective collective action, and formal heritage/voice regulation. Its next test is whether fan consultation changes documented ownership, stadium, identity, ticket and financial decisions, and whether those changes preserve local access, club survival and durable trust rather than treating stated action as realized influence.

**Latest household-expectations rotation — 2026-09-28:** the [ECB Consumer Expectations Survey August 2026 record](records/comparative-ecb-consumer-expectations-august-2026-income-credit-asymmetry.json) adds a distributional expectations route: lower-income euro-area respondents report higher inflation and unemployment expectations and expect mortgage rates 1.3 percentage points above the top income quintile, even as aggregate income, spending and credit-access expectations remain comparatively stable. Its next test is whether this expectation gap becomes realized differences in housing access, borrowing, major-purchase delay, savings drawdown, employment exposure, and political attribution. The survey supports the expectation layer, not those downstream outcomes.
| Broad event-ledger portfolio | 98 schema-valid ledgers; 302 events; 454 typed arrows, including 164 explicitly open arrows | [Ledger index](data/us-broad-event-ledger-index-v1.json), [portfolio summary](data/us-broad-event-ledger-portfolio-summary-v1.json), `validate_broad_event_ledger_portfolio_summary.py` |
| Findings | Domain findings plus a program-level long-form finding are generated as Markdown and HTML and parity-checked where the matched-evidence contract applies | [Matched-evidence index](us-matched-evidence-index.md), [program-level finding](findings/us-broad-program-unequal-optionality-path-001.md), `validate_us_finding_parity.py` |
| Evidence discipline | Units, dates, geography, denominators, methods, limits, counterexamples, and open arrows are required | [Trend protocol](US-BROAD-TREND-EXTRACTION-PROTOCOL_V1.md) |
| Link integrity | Relative Markdown references are checked across the workspace | `validate_local_markdown_links.py` |
| Source-record integrity | Project source-search records must carry date, geography, status, question, and linked source metadata | `validate_source_search_records.py` |
| Trend-record integrity | Recurring trend observations must preserve period, denominator, method, uncertainty, subgroup, counterinterpretation, source URL, and retrieval hash | `validate_trend_observation_records.py` |
| Trend metadata depth | Required fields are complete across all 937 records/2094 observations; optional related-source and reproduction-audit context is reported separately | [Trend metadata coverage audit](US-TREND-METADATA-COVERAGE-AUDIT_V1.md), `audit_trend_metadata_coverage.py` |
| Trend publication | Validated trend records have generated Markdown and HTML registry editions | [Trend-observation registry](../site/us-trend-observations.html), `build_us_trend_observation_registry.py` |
| Cross-source synthesis | The current evidence is periodically read across the full material-to-state chain without merging incompatible units | [Cross-source trend synthesis](US-CROSS-SOURCE-TREND-SYNTHESIS_V1.md) |
| Recurrent-source freshness | Official recurring releases, next checks, revision rules, and access-gated dependencies are tracked separately from findings | [Vintage watchlist](US-RECURRENT-SOURCE-VINTAGE-WATCHLIST_V1.md) |
| Trend-to-atlas linkage | Every machine-readable trend/case record is tagged to validated atlas umbrella themes | `theme_ids` in trend schema and [published registry](../site/us-trend-observations.html) |
| Theme breadth control | Trend records are grouped by umbrella theme and missing-record themes are surfaced | [Trend-theme coverage](../site/us-trend-theme-coverage.html), `build_us_trend_theme_coverage.py` |
| Control synchronization | Registry, theme coverage, source-packet count, HTML edition, and this ledger are checked against current source files | `validate_program_control_sync.py` |
| Active depth lane | Second-cycle same-person and event-compatible depth: timing, persistence, payment, coverage, institutional route, remedy, and household-room boundaries | [Next-pass queue](US-BROAD-NEXT-PASS-QUEUE_V1.md) |
| Hard acquisition dependency | PSID main waves and/or an equivalent same-unit event design | [PSID extract specification](projects/us-household-calendar-integration/psid-material-time-care-extract-spec-v1.md), [wave-file audit protocol](projects/us-household-calendar-integration/psid-wave-file-audit-protocol-v1.md) |

The current 1636-record registry comprises 1634
`us-trend-observation-record-v1` records and two explicitly accepted
compatibility-format records. The record validator reports this format
breakdown and rejects any unregistered format.

The latest U.S. energy-infrastructure rotation adds the [EIA Short-Term Energy Outlook record](records/us-eia-steo-september-2026-electricity-demand-data-centers-household-prices.json): electricity demand, data-center and manufacturing growth, geopolitical energy exposure, generation mix, emissions, and household-price pressure are connected while preserving the forecast and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. episode-level administrative rotation adds the [California Medi-Cal expansion access and continuity record](records/us-california-medical-expansion-access-continuity-2025.json): immigration-status-neutral eligibility is followed into reported enrollment and initial care use, then separated from renewal, re-entry, premium payment, treatment completion, household room, and trust outcomes. The episode preserves the policy, qualitative-sample, and future-rule boundaries rather than treating initial access as durable security.

The latest U.S. infrastructure and public-legitimacy rotation adds the [NYC congestion-pricing implementation record](records/us-nyc-congestion-pricing-implementation-2026.json): a named tolling intervention is followed into observed zone traffic, reported revenue, early bus-speed and ridership indicators, air-quality evaluation, and a capital project portfolio. Completed capital benefit, distributional incidence, route-level reliability, local voice, and durable legitimacy remain open.

The latest U.S. care-and-cultural-infrastructure rotation adds the [NYC universal school-meals record](records/us-nyc-universal-school-meals-access-learning-2026.json): universal eligibility is followed into historical student-level meal participation and learning estimates, current system scale, feedback routes, and operational food accountability. Current consumption, equal practical access, household food security, quality, and student voice remain open.

The latest U.S. household-security rotation adds the [California wildfire insurance and FAIR Plan record](records/us-california-wildfire-insurance-fair-plan-coverage-remedy-2026.json): wildfire exposure is followed into policy availability, premium burden, FAIR Plan dependence, catastrophe claims, payments, regulatory response, and early market-stabilization signals. Household claim adequacy, payment timing, repair, mobility, affordability, and durable recovery remain open.
The executable follow-up is the [California household-recovery acquisition map](projects/us-wildfire-insurance/california-household-recovery-acquisition-map-2026-09-29.md), which routes public claims, displacement, debris, and permitting surfaces into a bounded property/place ledger without asserting a same-household join.
Its first aggregate operating layer is the [LA County rebuilding dashboard record](../analysis/records/us-la-county-fire-rebuilding-dashboard-2026.json): current damage estimates and construction stages are now observed, while insurance adequacy, occupancy, household recovery, and stay/move remain open.

The latest cross-episode synthesis is [Conditional public capacity: when formal provision becomes lived legitimacy](projects/cross-domain/conditional-public-capacity-legitimacy-2026-09-28.md): Medi-Cal, congestion pricing, universal school meals, and wildfire insurance converge on a conditional-legitimacy test—reachability, usability, continuity, correction, and attribution—without being pooled into one causal estimate.

The latest U.S. institutional-legitimacy rotation adds the [Weidenbaum Center institutional-trust record](records/us-washu-weidenbaum-institutional-trust-party-place-pocketbooks-2026.json): trust is sorted by party, place, and economic security, with institution-specific partisan and urban-rural differences, while preserving the repeated-survey and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. consumer-infrastructure rotation adds the [Federal Reserve Diary of Consumer Payment Choice record](records/us-federal-reserve-diary-consumer-payment-choice-2026-cash-digital-rural-income.json): payment modernization, cash persistence, backup and store-of-value behavior, and age-, income-, and rurality-linked differences are connected while preserving the diary, repeated-cross-section, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. working-family rotation adds the [Pew working parents record](records/us-pew-working-parents-2026-work-family-childcare-flexibility.json): family employment arrangements, employer flexibility, childcare cost and availability, remote-work overlap, and gendered care allocation are connected while preserving the survey, conditional-denominator, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest adaptive-resilience rotation adds the [World Bank Malawi INSPIRE-O record](records/comparative-worldbank-malawi-inspire-o-adaptive-social-protection-climate-jobs-2026.json): climate and economic shocks are connected to adaptive cash protection, climate-smart public works, women’s and youth employment, digital registries, payment infrastructure, targeting, transparency, and household resilience. It is an announced project design, not evidence of implementation or outcomes.

The latest U.S. housing-structure rotation adds the [Harvard JCHS State of the Nation’s Housing 2026 record](records/us-harvard-jchs-housing-supply-mobility-immigration-2026.json): low-cost supply, construction prices, residential mobility, migration, household formation, first-time buying, and cost burden are connected while preserving the secondary-synthesis and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative digital-government rotation adds the [OECD Digital Government Outlook 2026 service-fragmentation record](records/comparative-oecd-digital-government-outlook-2026-service-fragmentation-ai-capacity.json): public-service reachability, cross-agency life journeys, implementation feedback, digital infrastructure, AI adoption, interoperability, and accountability are connected while preserving the comparative-report and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. platform-consumer rotation adds the [World Happiness Report 2026 product-traps record](records/comparative-world-happiness-social-media-product-traps-2026.json): willingness to use, willingness to exit, social-network dependence, time valuation, wellbeing, non-user externalities, and collective coordination are connected while preserving the experimental-study and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. hidden-work rotation adds the [GAO Unpaid Household Work record](records/us-gao-unpaid-household-work-time-value-care-2026.json): unpaid domestic labor, caregiving, sandwich care, time scarcity, gender and employment differences, and national economic value are connected while preserving the ATUS diary and imputed-valuation boundaries.

The latest U.S. time-allocation rotation adds the [BLS American Time Use Survey 2025 record](records/us-bls-atus-2025-time-allocation-work-care-leisure.json): remote-work access, household production, gendered time, childcare, socializing, television, computer leisure, and the composition of daily life are connected while preserving the annual time-diary and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative fiscal-capacity rotation adds the [IMF Social Protection Efficiency record](records/comparative-imf-social-protection-efficiency-2026-inequality-fiscal-space.json): spending, targeting, transfer coverage, inequality reduction, poverty, aging, fiscal space, and political tradeoffs are connected while preserving the working-paper and model-based boundaries.

The latest comparative human-capital rotation adds the [World Bank Human Capital Report 2026 record](records/comparative-worldbank-human-capital-settings-2026-homes-neighborhoods-workplaces.json): home care, neighborhood exposure, workplace learning, gendered labor participation, job quality, and future earnings are connected while preserving the modeled-index and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. civic-culture rotation adds the [Pew-Knight public-life engagement record](records/us-pew-civic-engagement-public-life-2026.json): news attention, voting, volunteering, religious and community participation, direct political contact, socioeconomic position, and institutional feedback are connected while preserving the descriptive cluster and aggregate-survey boundaries.

The current comparative political-voice synthesis foregrounds the [canonical OECD Trust Survey political-voice record](records/comparative-oecd-trust-2026-political-voice-responsiveness-barriers.json): voting, low-cost expression, information sources, perceived influence, barriers, institutional responsiveness, and trust are connected while preserving the cross-national survey and perception-based boundaries.

The latest comparative identity-infrastructure rotation adds the [UK Digital Identity Sectoral Analysis 2026 record](records/comparative-uk-digital-identity-sector-2026-trust-access-market.json): market expansion, public-service and employment verification, income-linked understanding and use, context-specific physical/digital preference, privacy, exclusion, and legitimacy are connected while preserving the modeled-market and survey boundaries.

The latest comparative demographic-transformation rotation adds the [European Commission JRC Demographic Transformation 2026 record](records/comparative-eu-jrc-demographic-transformation-2026-ageing-work-care-housing-migration.json): population decline, longevity, care demand, labor participation, youth transitions, skilled migration, housing, regional cohesion, and multilevel policy response are connected while preserving the projection and policy-announcement boundaries.

The latest comparative household-consumption rotation adds the [Eurostat household expenditure record](records/comparative-eurostat-household-consumption-2026-essential-culture-transport.json): essential budget concentration, relative movement in culture, hospitality, education, transport, and information/communication shares, price exposure, and the limits of aggregate consumer inference are connected while preserving the macro-expenditure boundaries.

The latest comparative migration-governance rotation adds the [MPI Europe control-signalling record](records/comparative-mpi-europe-migration-control-signalling-integration-trust-2026.json): visible toughness, housing and language support, family separation, labor-market integration, public-service capacity, community voice, demographic planning, and trust are connected while preserving the heterogeneous policy-synthesis boundaries.

The latest water-infrastructure rotation adds the [World Bank Brazil Paraíba water-security record](records/comparative-worldbank-brazil-paraiba-water-security-sanitation-jobs-2026.json): drought frequency, poverty, water losses, sanitation gaps, rural service access, state capacity, PPP governance, jobs, household time, and cost are connected in one place-based infrastructure route. It is a project announcement with estimates, not evidence of completed service or welfare outcomes.

The latest stability-to-work rotation adds the [World Bank Zimbabwe Growth and Jobs record](records/comparative-worldbank-zimbabwe-growth-jobs-informality-infrastructure-2026.json): macroeconomic stabilization and GDP growth are separated from productive employment, household income, formalization, power reliability, infrastructure, and worker earnings. It is a country-report summary with modeled scenarios, not a causal reform evaluation.

The latest small-island growth rotation adds the [World Bank Seychelles Growth and Jobs record](records/comparative-worldbank-seychelles-growth-jobs-digital-skills-2026.json): aging, skills shortages, geographic isolation, labor-market reform, youth transitions, digital affordability, public services, jobs, wages, and household purchasing power are connected while preserving the modeled-scenario boundary.

The latest U.S. consumer-culture rotation adds the [National Restaurant Association Consumer Insights record](records/us-national-restaurant-association-consumer-insights-2026-affordability-occasions-k-shaped-spending.json): financial strain coexists with protected restaurant occasions, trading down, grocery substitution, generational and income divergence, operator margins, and workforce pressure. It is industry-sponsored tracking with incomplete public methodology, not a representative household panel.

The latest geoeconomic-development rotation adds the [World Bank Djibouti private-investment record](records/comparative-worldbank-djibouti-private-investment-energy-digital-tourism-2026.json): strategic location and connectivity are connected to energy cost, off-grid solar, data centers, tourism, skills, regulation, cultural assets, and potential local jobs. It is a Country Private Sector Diagnostic summary with investment potentials, not evidence of realized capital or broad-based gains.

The latest U.S. food-trust-and-health rotation adds the [IFIC Food & Health Survey record](records/us-ific-food-health-survey-2026-food-safety-health-beliefs-labels.json): food-supply confidence, ingredient and processing beliefs, label awareness, federalism preferences, and household food-safety practice are connected while preserving the weighted online-panel and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global cultural-economy rotation adds the [UNESCO Re|Shaping Policies for Creativity record](records/comparative-unesco-reshaping-policies-creativity-2026-cultural-work-platforms-freedom.json): cultural policy recognition, trade, digital creator income, platform visibility, skills, gender inclusion, public funding, and artistic freedom are connected while preserving the country-reporting, sector-statistical, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global youth-futures rotation adds the [World Economic Forum Youth Pulse record](records/comparative-wef-youth-pulse-2026-cost-ai-community-trust-purpose.json): inequality and financial strain are connected to purpose-driven work, education mismatch, AI adoption, entry-level job anxiety, community-level trust, political participation, climate concern, and youth-led action while preserving the publisher-survey and selected-case boundaries.

The latest global democracy-and-conflict rotation adds the [International IDEA Global State of Democracy record](records/comparative-international-idea-gsod-2026-democracy-conflict-rule-of-law.json): democratic stagnation, rights and rule-of-law erosion, participation, state capacity, public accountability, and conflict risk are connected while preserving the index, event-tracker, comparative, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global consumer-culture rotation adds the [NIQ and World Data Lab Two Consumers record](records/comparative-niq-world-data-lab-2026-two-consumers-premium-value-mindsets.json): inflation, unequal spending power, situational premiumization, trade-down, trust, identity, convenience, private label, and regional divergence are connected while preserving the commercial-data, survey, projection, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative household-security rotation adds the [PPRC Bangladesh State of the Real Economy record](records/comparative-pprc-bangladesh-real-economy-2026-household-fragility-resilience.json): poverty, vulnerable non-poor status, budget room, cumulative shocks, informal work, debt, food insecurity, health costs, coping networks, digital participation, social protection, and accountable governance are connected while preserving the nationally representative survey and non-causal boundaries.

The latest Asia-Pacific deprivation-measurement rotation adds the [ADB Key Indicators 2026 record](records/comparative-adb-key-indicators-asia-pacific-2026-within-household-deprivation.json): household versus individual poverty, gender and rural inequality, life-stage exposure, health insurance, internet access, property ownership, decision power, and targeted policy are connected while preserving the comparative-indicator and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative urban-cohesion rotation adds the [KAS South Africa migration, rule-of-law, and social-cohesion record](records/comparative-kas-south-africa-migration-rule-law-social-cohesion-2026.json): unemployment, inequality, migration, housing, public services, institutional trust, xenophobic mobilisation, rule of law, and social cohesion are connected while preserving the qualitative-case, cited-statistics, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. administrative-trust rotation adds the [Urban Institute immigrant-families record](records/us-urban-immigrant-families-enforcement-chilling-effect-2025.json): immigration concern is connected to practical non-use of work, health care, schools, police, community life, and safety-net programs, with sharper exposure in mixed-status families while preserving the repeated-cross-sectional and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. AI-work rotation adds the [Census HTOPS AI task/time record](records/us-census-htops-ai-work-task-time-savings-2026.json): workplace AI adoption is connected to task use, reported time savings, and unequal access by education, sex, and age while preserving the weighted cross-sectional and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative cultural-infrastructure rotation adds the [Canada Arts and Heritage Access survey record](records/comparative-canada-arts-heritage-access-availability-2025-26-belonging-cost.json): arts participation, socializing, welcome, belonging, contribution, and local-economic value are connected while preserving cost, survey, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative political-voice rotation adds the [OECD political voice and trust record](records/comparative-oecd-trust-2026-political-voice-responsiveness-barriers.json): voting, consultation, participation barriers, perceived official responsiveness, material resources, and institutional trust are connected while preserving the country-average and non-causal boundaries.

The latest comparative household-exposure rotation adds the [ONS Great Britain cost-of-living and shortages record](records/comparative-ons-great-britain-cost-living-conflict-shortages-may-2026.json): price pressure, emergency liquidity, food and energy substitution, goods availability, mobility, and international-conflict worry are connected while preserving the short-period survey and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global consumer-protection rotation adds the [OECD Consumer Finance Risk Monitor record](records/comparative-oecd-consumer-finance-risk-monitor-2026-scams-digital-exclusion-remedy.json): scams, digital exclusion, macro pressure, product detriment, complaints, supervisory coordination, and consumer remedy are connected while preserving the authority-assessment and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global social-connection policy rotation adds the [194-state loneliness-policy scoping review](records/comparative-global-loneliness-policy-scoping-review-194-states-2026.json): national recognition, community infrastructure, cross-sector action, funding, and evaluation are connected while preserving the policy-document and non-causal boundaries.

The latest U.S. housing-security rotation adds the [Federal Reserve housing record](records/us-federal-reserve-household-housing-affordability-insurance-2025.json): tenure, income, payment burden, renter arrears, forced mobility, insurance affordability, weather exposure, and household balance-sheet risk are connected while preserving the survey and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding comparative household-security rotation adds the [UN SDG Goal 1 2026 record](records/comparative-un-sdg1-2026-poverty-protection-spending-aid.json): extreme poverty, social-protection coverage, benefit-type inequality, public-spending incidence, working poverty, aid retrenchment, and state buffering are connected while preserving the aggregate-indicator and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding comparative cultural-adaptation rotation adds the [Korea Social and Cultural Trends 2026 record](records/comparative-korea-social-cultural-trends-2026-adaptation-consumption-ai-kculture.json): AI governance, hyper-personalization, practical consumption, wellness, K-culture, fandom, emotional value, and coexistence are connected while preserving the online-keyword and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding comparative care-infrastructure rotation adds the [UN resilient care and support systems record](records/comparative-un-care-support-systems-2026-poverty-dignity-work-family.json): life-course care, household time, poverty, gendered unpaid work, social protection, workforce capacity, public services, and dignity are connected while preserving the policy-note and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding comparative labor-market rotation adds the [ILO Employment and Social Trends 2026 record](records/comparative-ilo-employment-social-trends-2026-job-quality-inequality.json): headline employment stability, the global jobs gap, informality, working poverty, productivity divergence, gender and youth exclusion, trade exposure, AI risk, and domestic institutional buffering are connected while preserving the global-projection and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding U.S. disaster-resilience rotation adds the [GAO BRIC resilience-grant record](records/us-gao-bric-resilience-grant-delay-communication-outcomes-2026.json): award status, review delay, reimbursement, policy reversal, communication, local capacity, planning support, and outcome measurement are connected while preserving the audit, selected-interview, and non-causal boundaries.

The preceding U.S. local-capacity rotation adds the [NLC Municipal Infrastructure Conditions record](records/us-nlc-municipal-infrastructure-conditions-2026-local-capacity-funding.json): infrastructure condition, property-tax dependence, cash-based finance, staff capacity, federal grants, and public-system reliability are connected while preserving the municipal-survey and non-causal boundaries.

The latest geopolitical-institutional rotation adds the [Salzburg Global public-institutions record](records/comparative-salzburg-global-public-institutions-2026-transactional-order-state-capacity.json): a more transactional international order and thinner public capacity are connected to alliance diversification, private dependence, AI literacy, distributed foresight, long-term commitment, actionable inclusion, and institutional trust. It is a curated expert synthesis, not representative public opinion or a causal evaluation of state capacity.

The latest civic-political rotation adds the [USC Schaeffer Index record](records/comparative-usc-schaeffer-index-2026-civic-association-populism-20-countries.json):
civic association, friendship, bridging contact, political diversity, belonging,
financial disappointment, and populist-nationalist support are treated as
connected but non-causal stages.

The latest fiscal-legitimacy rotation adds the [OECD The People and the Budget record](records/comparative-oecd-people-budget-2026-fiscal-understanding-legitimacy.json): fiscal repair depends partly on whether people can understand, contest, and shape difficult trade-offs. The report links low trust, limited perceived voice, fragmented information environments, citizen engagement, and the public role of independent fiscal institutions. It is an OECD framework and survey synthesis, not a causal test of communication or participation.

The latest geopolitical-trade rotation adds the [IMF Annual Report 2026 trade-resilience record](records/comparative-imf-annual-report-2026-trade-resilience-supply-chain-geopolitics.json): geopolitical shocks are reorganizing trade, energy, food, and supply chains, while resilience requires a contested tradeoff between redundancy and efficiency. It is an IMF institutional synthesis, not a household-incidence or causal trade evaluation.

The latest cultural-economy rotation adds the [European Audiovisual Observatory Key Trends 2026 record](records/comparative-eao-key-trends-2026-cultural-work-streaming-public-media.json): cultural production is a large employment and consumer system, but freelance insecurity, platform concentration, uneven public-media funding, and transatlantic revenue power shape who can create, distribute, and access culture. It is a sectoral and market-data synthesis, not a creator panel or audience-welfare evaluation.

The latest platform-governance rotation adds the [ILO Platform Economy Convention No. 193 record](records/comparative-ilo-platform-economy-convention-2026-algorithmic-management-rights-remedy.json): the first dedicated global labor standard makes platform scope, worker classification, pay, social security, algorithmic decisions, data, deactivation, remedy, enforcement, and cross-border implementation explicit. It is an adopted normative instrument, not evidence of ratification, compliance, or worker outcomes.

The preceding supply-chain resilience rotation adds the [UK Government Office for Science global supply chains record](records/comparative-uk-government-science-global-supply-chains-foresight-2026-network-risk-resilience.json): multi-tier network concentration, climate, geopolitics, chokepoints, and adaptation choices shape the path from global disruption to national security, affordability, and household exposure. It is a foresight study with modelling and scenarios, not a disruption forecast or household-incidence estimate.

The preceding urban-capacity rotation adds the [OECD Inclusive Growth in Cities record](records/comparative-oecd-inclusive-growth-cities-2026-within-city-opportunity-gaps.json): more than 600 EU cities show that productivity and GDP growth do not automatically reduce poverty or expand opportunity; mobility, housing, services, climate action, participation, granular data, and cross-department delivery determine whether growth becomes shared capacity. It is a comparative city synthesis with selected intervention evidence, not a universal causal estimate.

The preceding urban-capacity rotation adds the [World Bank Madagascar Urbanization Review record](records/comparative-worldbank-madagascar-urbanization-growth-poverty-infrastructure-2026.json): cities generate substantial economic output while poverty, informal settlement, weak water and waste systems, climate exposure, and limited municipal finance constrain inclusive growth. It is a country review and public summary, not a longitudinal household panel or causal reform evaluation.

The preceding household-shock rotation adds the [World Bank Trade Policy and Natural Shocks Dashboard record](records/comparative-worldbank-trade-natural-shocks-household-income-distribution-2025.json): climate yields, conflict-linked food inflation, and trade restrictions reach household income and food security through unequal exposure and response channels. It is a modeled cross-country dashboard, not respondent-level longitudinal evidence or a universal causal estimate.

The latest comparative public-service rotation adds the [Norway Citizen Survey 2026 record](records/comparative-norway-dfo-citizen-survey-2026-service-voice-ai-trust.json): reasonably usable public services coexist with weaker perceived influence, declining trust in public administration, low expectations that complaints produce improvement, and skepticism that public-sector AI will treat groups fairly. It is an official weighted survey and repeated cross-sectional comparison, not a causal service or AI evaluation.

The preceding fiscal-capacity rotation adds the [OECD Restoring Public Finances record](records/comparative-oecd-restoring-public-finances-2026-social-protection-tradeoffs.json): debt, aging, defence, health, and crisis legacies are pushing savings into social protection, healthcare, public operations, and workforce design, making fiscal repair a distributional and legitimacy problem. It is an OECD fiscal synthesis and government survey, not a household-incidence or causal reform evaluation.

The preceding public-service delivery rotation adds the [OECD Serving Citizens 2026 record](records/comparative-oecd-serving-citizens-2026-life-events-feedback-implementation.json): administrative encounters are treated as the everyday interface between life-course needs, public rights, institutional trust, and service integration, while strategy, feedback, and data-sharing gaps remain visible. It is a government-survey and service-management assessment, not a citizen panel or causal evaluation.

The preceding measurement-infrastructure rotation adds the [UN DESA National Inclusion Accounts record](records/comparative-un-desa-national-inclusion-accounts-2026-life-course-distribution.json): age-specific economic flows can be further disaggregated by education, income, family type, residence, and migration status to expose resource, care, and public-transfer differences hidden by national averages. It is a methodological capacity-development manual, not a population estimate or policy-impact evaluation.

The preceding life-course well-being rotation adds the [OECD Finland well-being record](records/comparative-oecd-finland-life-course-wellbeing-2026-intergenerational-loneliness.json): strong aggregate national performance coexists with youth transition and mental-health fragility, middle-aged male employment decline, unequal unpaid work, loneliness across age groups, and demographic-fiscal pressure. It is a working-paper country assessment, not a causal policy evaluation.

The preceding growth-to-well-being rotation adds the [OECD Korea well-being record](records/comparative-oecd-korea-wellbeing-2026-growth-life-course-social-capital.json): long-run gains in life expectancy, safety, education, income, and child poverty coexist with fragile youth independence, long work hours, loneliness, high suicide, gender barriers, declining trust, and environmental costs. It is a multidimensional country assessment, not a causal growth-model evaluation.

The preceding displacement-to-work rotation adds the [World Bank Kenya refugee-host jobs record](records/comparative-worldbank-kenya-refugee-host-jobs-aid-shrinkage-2026.json): aid contraction, weak labor markets, legal work barriers, food insecurity, climate shocks, and shared public-service constraints are connected across refugees and host communities. It is a place-based longitudinal case synthesis, not a universal refugee-outcome estimate or Shirika Plan evaluation.

The preceding food-and-connection rotation adds the [OECD food social-connectedness record](records/comparative-oecd-food-social-connectedness-2026-community-infrastructure.json): food access, shared meals, cooking, gardening, cultural meaning, volunteer work, and repurposed spaces are treated as low-stigma social infrastructure. It is a case-study and policy synthesis, not a causal programme evaluation.

The preceding youth-mental-health rotation adds the [OECD youth mental-health record](records/comparative-oecd-youth-mental-health-2026-intersecting-pressure-prevention.json): worsening youth mental health is framed as cumulative pressure across digital life, school, family, housing, economic security, climate, conflict, and social connection, requiring prevention and low-threshold support. It is a cross-country synthesis and policy assessment, not a pooled causal estimate.

The preceding AI-divergence rotation adds the [UNDP Next Great Divergence record](records/comparative-undp-next-great-divergence-2026-ai-capability-inequality.json): AI’s effects are being framed as a capability-distribution problem spanning infrastructure, skills, work, gender, public services, governance, trust, and environmental costs. It is a regional human-development assessment and policy roadmap, not a causal forecast of inequality.

The preceding anticipatory-capacity rotation adds the [World Bank AI social-risk forecasting record](records/comparative-worldbank-ai-social-risk-forecasting-2026-anticipatory-capacity.json): AI and machine learning are being connected to conflict, displacement, crime, and poverty forecasting so social protection and public-service capacity can move ahead of shocks. It is a proof-of-concept and operational-framing source, not evidence that prediction improves welfare or is bias-free.

The preceding migration-research rotation adds the [European Commission future-research record](records/comparative-eu-future-research-migration-integration-2026-policy-capacity.json): migration research is being organized as shared policy infrastructure across integration, labor sustainability, legal pathways, AI, climate mobility, data systems, and practical implementation. It is an agenda-setting and research-capacity source, not an outcome evaluation.

The latest labor-mobility rotation adds the [WEC Social Impact Report 2026 record](records/comparative-wec-social-impact-2026-borderless-talent-mobility-safeguards.json): labor shortages and underused talent are connected through international matching, qualification recognition, language and skills support, legal pathways, integration, and worker safeguards. It is industry-association and case-based evidence, not a causal migration or worker-outcome evaluation.

The latest climate-risk rotation adds the [WorldRiskReport 2026 record](records/comparative-worldriskreport-2026-storms-vulnerability-social-protection.json): storm risk is converted into disaster through exposure, social vulnerability, warning reach, infrastructure, health access, coping capacity, adaptation, and social protection. It is a 193-country structural index and thematic report, not a short-term event forecast or household-loss estimate.

The latest global-health architecture rotation adds the [KFF 14-institution
mapping record](records/comparative-kff-global-health-architecture-2026-institutions-financing-civil-society.json):
governance voice, mandate overlap, technical assistance, financing cycles,
graduation, co-financing, and country sustainability are treated as distinct
institutional-capacity stages.

The latest public-sector AI rotation adds the [BCG Digital Government Citizen
Survey record](records/comparative-bcg-digital-government-citizen-survey-2026-ai-services-human-oversight.json):
citizen AI use, agentic comfort, human oversight, appeals, public reporting,
and regional variation are treated as a public-service legitimacy route rather
than as proof of safe or equitable implementation.

The latest government-technology rotation adds the [Gartner government
technology trends record](records/comparative-gartner-government-technology-trends-2026-agentic-ai-cyber-workforce.json):
agentic AI, adaptive cyber resilience, digital identity, workforce readiness,
service redesign, and accountability are treated as linked institutional

The latest community-prosperity rotation adds the [Prosper Global Voices of

shared wellbeing, safety, future planning, local knowledge, mutual support,

The latest volunteerism rotation adds the [UNV State of the World’s

volunteer action, mutual aid, trust, collective problem-solving, crisis

The latest belonging-and-place rotation adds the [YMCA Canada Connections
Snapshot record](records/comparative-ymca-canada-connections-snapshot-2026-third-places-belonging.json):
third places, affordability, welcome, time, social discomfort, and entry-point

The latest care-infrastructure rotation adds the [Statistics Canada home-based

provider capacity, waitlists, schedule flexibility, disability accommodation,

The latest migration-and-work rotation adds the [Statistics Canada childhood-

certification, trade sorting, union and pension coverage, mobility, earnings

between childhood arrival and durable economic inclusion.

The latest cultural-consumer rotation adds the [England Community and

physical and digital participation, affordability, deprivation, local access,
life stage and socioeconomic position are treated as the conversion conditions
between cultural interest and actual participation.

The latest mobility-capacity rotation adds the [Statistics Canada interprovincial
labour-mobility record](records/comparative-statcan-interprovincial-labour-mobility-estimates-2026.json):
occupational recognition, relocation costs, predicted mobility, regional labor

The latest consumer-room rotation adds the [INSEE France September consumer-

confidence, saving intentions, major-purchase restraint, price perceptions and

The latest cultural-consumer rotation adds the [Ipsos B&A Sign of the Times

The latest mental-load rotation adds the [YouGov Nordic mental-load record](records/comparative-yougov-nordic-mental-load-2026-gender-wellbeing-help-seeking.json):
invisible planning, gendered wellbeing, financial pressure, help-seeking, and
workplace boundaries are treated as a structural support-conversion problem.

The latest place-based rotation adds the [Nordregio State of the Nordic Region

The latest regional-well-being rotation adds the [OECD Regional Well-Being record](records/comparative-oecd-regional-wellbeing-2026-eleven-dimensions-place-trust-policy-targeting.json): eleven dimensions, regional comparison, within-country disparity measurement, and place-based service and governance capacity are treated as an explanatory layer for why national trends reach places differently.

The latest information-authority rotation adds the [AP-NORC/API Media Insight record](records/us-ap-norc-media-insight-2026-influencers-news-trust-generational-authority.json): generational creator use, topic-specific discovery, source confidence, AI comparison, misinformation attribution, and news avoidance are treated as a distributed-authority route rather than as a simple decline-of-news finding.

The latest cultural-consumption rotation adds the [DIMA 2026 music-streaming record](records/comparative-dima-music-streaming-2026-discovery-spend-local-culture-ai.json): streaming engagement, spending, discovery, back-catalogue use, local culture, AI-generated music, and regulation are treated as a platform-to-culture route while preserving the trade-association boundary.

The latest youth-culture rotation adds the [Mastercard Next Growth Generation record](records/comparative-mastercard-youth-culture-2026-genalpha-agency-creators-fandom-ai.json): Gen Z and Gen Alpha agency, creators, fandoms, micro-communities, AI co-pilots, household influence, and projected economic power are treated as a youth-to-culture route while preserving the sponsor-research and projection boundaries.

The latest wellbeing-consumption rotation adds the [Kantar Pursuit of Wellbeing record](records/comparative-kantar-pursuit-wellbeing-2026-affordability-agency-care-ai.json): affordability, health behavior, institutional reliability, care navigation, personalization, and AI guidance are treated as a consumer-led wellbeing route while preserving the commercial-foresight boundary.

The latest Nordic consumer-culture rotation adds the [WPP Media 2026 consumer-trends record](records/comparative-wpp-media-nordic-consumer-trends-2026-mental-scarcity-status-control.json): mental scarcity, life-stage pressure, guided choice, and status through time and control are treated as a commercial-foresight route while preserving its Nordic, publisher-produced, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest cultural-AI rotation adds the [Google Research cultural GenAI record](records/comparative-google-research-cultural-genai-2026-identity-redlines.json): cultural identity, sacred meaning, historical memory, AI familiarity, representation red lines, participation, and recourse are treated as a global cultural-governance route while preserving the preprint, survey, and non-causal boundaries.

The latest global workplace-culture rotation adds the [SHRM Global Workplace Culture record](records/comparative-shrm-global-workplace-culture-2026-organizational-types-decision-making.json): organizational culture types, decision rights, work systems, relationships, adaptability, and strategy alignment are treated as a work-control route while preserving the publisher-research and non-causal boundaries.

The latest public-pessimism rotation adds the [FGS Global Radar 2026 record](records/comparative-fgs-global-radar-2026-pessimism-disruption-affordability.json): institutional distrust, reform demand, rule-breaking leadership, elite perceptions, affordability, fairness, and visible results are treated as a political-legitimacy route while preserving the publisher-polling and non-causal boundaries.

The latest consumer-institution rotation adds the [European consumer-policy and trust record](records/comparative-europe-consumer-policy-trust-vulnerability-2026.json): consumer-policy capacity, market norms, consumer trust, economic vulnerability, and uneven recourse reach are treated as an institutional consumer-protection route while preserving the observational and non-causal boundaries.

The latest identity-and-belonging rotation adds the [WGS/Strategy& national identity record](records/comparative-wgs-strategyand-national-identity-2026-belonging-trust-resilience.json): identity markers, civic expression, participation, trust, resilience, competitiveness, and Gulf policy examples are treated as a cultural-state route while preserving the strategic-framework and non-causal boundaries.

## Current temporary-input boundary

The crash-safe recheck on 2026-09-19 found no temporary MEPS staging directory
under `/tmp/cgtm-meps-2024/`, no 2025 SIPP archive under
`/tmp/us-broad-sipp-2025/`, and no temporary UAS codebook at the previously
recorded path. Their historical hashes, bounded derived outputs, and research
writeups remain in the repository, but these inputs are not currently available
for reruns. No current estimate should be described as freshly reproducible
from those temporary files until an authorized restoration or reacquisition is
completed. The next authorized pass begins with file inventory, hashes, keys,
weights, timing, and missingness checks.

## Broad-goal resumption checkpoint

**Reset:** 2026-09-16

The program is explicitly resumed as the full societal, cultural, political,
consumer, institutional, firm, infrastructure, and geopolitical trend-mining
program. The household constraint and health-cost work remains an evidence
lane, but its unavailable UAS/PSID respondent files do not pause the atlas or
define its success condition.

The first rotating pass used already acquired local evidence and required no
bulk download. It will read the CFPB complaint and response layers, household
fraud and recovery estimates, CPSC safety intervention records, and platform
remedy cases as one bounded comparison of **practical exit**:

```text
problem or restriction
  -> visibility and route to a decision-maker
  -> effort, waiting, review, correction, or compensation
  -> restored access or usable alternative
  -> continued use, switching, non-use, trust, or exit
```

The comparison is not a pooled consumer-outcome estimate. Each source keeps
its own unit, denominator, clock, and evidence type. The deliverable is a
cross-domain mechanism synthesis that identifies which middle stages are
observed and which final outcomes remain open. The next rotation then moves to
AI/platform agency and work control, followed by public-system feedback and
firm/infrastructure dependence, while material/time/care remains an active
later lane rather than a program-wide gate.

The completed first-pass artifact is the [practical-exit cross-domain
synthesis](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/practical-exit-cross-domain-synthesis-v1.md).

The second rotation now advances AI/platform agency through the [AI optionality
and control-conversion synthesis](projects/us-digital-habits-attention/ai-optionality-control-conversion-synthesis-v1.md).
It preserves the distinction between adoption, immediate time or engagement
effects, captured benefit, dependence, correction, trust, and practical exit.

The third rotation advances public-system feedback through the [SNAP
route-divergence synthesis](projects/us-safety-net-access/snap-route-divergence-food-security-political-feedback-synthesis-v1.md).
It keeps administrative handling, benefit transition, food security, trust,
blame, and political action as separate stages and uses the new reproducible
reason-specific food-security layer without claiming a benefit effect.

The fourth rotation advances firm, infrastructure, and geopolitical capacity
through the [capacity-without-exit cross-domain synthesis](projects/ai-work-control/capacity-without-exit-cross-domain-synthesis-v1.md).
It keeps physical capacity, fiscal revenue, governance rules, local incidence,
replaceability, and external response as separate stages rather than treating
investment or an agreement as public benefit or leverage.
The current realization-stage audit now makes the comparison executable across
the US data-center and Poland JASSM-ER ledgers: the data-center case has 13
dated records including governance and operational-stress events, while the
JASSM-ER case has 7 records centered on authorization, agreement, planned
delivery, and a control comparator. Accepted output, replaceability, public or
partner incidence, and changed external behavior remain open.
The supplier-control follow-up now audits the richer local USAspending response:
74 returned subaward rows resolve to 51 named recipients, with descriptions,
UEIs, and performance-location fields on the returned records; the top five
recipients represent 49.5004% of returned amount. This identifies a traceable
network and a concentration lead, not production output, delivery, market
share, or replaceability.

The fifth rotation returns to material/time/care through the [time-as-hidden-
price cross-domain synthesis](projects/us-household-calendar-integration/time-as-hidden-price-cross-domain-synthesis-v1.md).
It uses existing SIPP, ATUS, MEPS, and SHED evidence without making the
unavailable PSID/UAS acquisition a program-wide gate, and keeps money, time,
care, health, recovery, trust, and political availability as distinct clocks.

The sixth rotation advances unequal exposure and cultural meaning through the
[unequal-optionality/status/meaning synthesis](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/unequal-optionality-status-meaning-synthesis-v1.md).
It compares subgroup-conditioned material and time surfaces with the HTOPS
political counterexample, keeping exposure, practical alternatives,
interpretation, trust, and action as separate stages.

The seventh rotation advances political judgment and collective action through
the [political-action menu synthesis](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/political-action-menu-not-ladder-synthesis-v1.md).
It treats voting, contact, volunteering, complaint, organizing, switching,
and withdrawal as distinct actions rather than one engagement ladder.
The latest Pew public-life layer sharpens this boundary by separating
Mobilizers, Connectors, Spectators, and Outsiders across political, civic,
religious, and news activity. The clusters show different action styles, not a
single scale from inactive to active; the same-person route from material or
institutional exposure through interpretation, chosen action, response, and
later legitimacy remains open.

The eighth rotation advances the place and local-capacity lane through [local
capacity is not practical exit](projects/us-local-business-place/place-capacity-practical-exit-cross-domain-synthesis-v1.md).
It reframes capacity as an option stack: presence must be joined to
reachability, usability, replaceability, recourse, and practical exit before a
place can be treated as protective. This uses existing local-business,
health-capacity, mobility, housing/insurance, public-route, consumer-recourse,
and place-meaning artifacts without pooling their incompatible units. The next
test remains a dated resident route or place-time episode with attempted use,
fallback, protected/sacrificed outcome, and later stay, switching, movement, or
action.

The ninth rotation advances financial capacity and household room through the
[macro-to-household financial-capacity synthesis](projects/us-financial-intermediation/macro-to-household-financial-capacity-synthesis-v1.md).
It keeps national growth, financial-system capacity, provider supply, user
access, household liquidity, debt, public monitoring, and political meaning as
separate stages. The key conclusion is that aggregate or provider capacity is
not automatically usable household room; the next test remains a dated
obligation or account event with alternatives, payment/record consequences,
remedy, later security, trust, or exit.

The tenth rotation advances housing security and the cost of staying put
through the [housing, insurance, hazard, and energy synthesis](projects/us-housing-insurance-risk/cost-of-staying-put-synthesis-v1.md).
It keeps rent arrears, insurance gaps, premiums, nonrenewal, hazard, energy
burden, repair, and public backstops as distinct but potentially compounding
layers. The next test remains a matched property or household episode linking
notice, claim or repair, assistance, payment/borrowing, health or work
trade-offs, and stay, move, or recovery.

The eleventh rotation advances the institutional-mediation lane through
[route-specific institutional friction](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/institutional-friction-route-specific-outcomes-cross-domain-synthesis-v1.md).
It separates prescription delay, authorization denial, benefit interruption,
complaint routing, and downstream debt remedies rather than treating them as
one friction measure. The next test is a same-case ledger with the route
object, timing, alternatives, effort, verified remedy, protected/sacrificed
outcome, and later trust, action, switching, or exit.

The twelfth rotation advances domestic capability, dependence, and state
leverage through the [domestic capacity and state-leverage bridge](projects/ai-work-control/domestic-capacity-dependence-state-leverage-cross-source-bridge-v1.md).
It keeps domestic affordability, firm and infrastructure capacity, ownership,
supplier choice, procurement, production, delivery, alliance implementation,
and external behavior as separate stages. The promotion rule is explicit:
spending, agreement, or capacity is not leverage until an observed refusal,
switch, negotiation, regulatory response, or changed external behavior shows
that another actor's options or conduct changed.

The thirteenth rotation advances cultural and political conversion through
[material pressure and political meaning](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/material-pressure-to-political-meaning-synthesis-v1.md).
It preserves the same-respondent temporal evidence for later food, energy, and
work outcomes while showing that institutional confidence, attribution,
identity, and civic action do not move automatically with hardship. The next
test remains a timed design that measures the responsible actor, prior
judgment, information environment, direct action, remedy, and later
legitimacy.

The fourteenth rotation advances time, care, work, and political availability
through the [time-and-care political-availability bridge](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/time-care-to-political-availability-bridge-v1.md).
It keeps work schedules, care, travel, illness, registration barriers, formal
representation, civic action, and withdrawal as distinct resources. The next
test remains a same-person work or care event with schedule control,
information, trust, attribution, voting or organizing, and employer/agency
response measured over time.

The fifteenth rotation advances the end-to-end depth lane through the [MEPS
event-to-later-panel follow-up](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-event-six-month-followup-v1.md).
It places first office, emergency-room, or inpatient events strictly between
R3/1 and R4/2 before same-person R5/3 health and employment outcomes. The
result strengthens the time-order and persistence stages, while preserving the
boundary that later status is not verified recovery: claim identity,
treatment continuity, alternatives, remedy, household trade-offs, trust, and
exit remain open.

The [February 2025 HTOPS linkage recheck](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/htops-2025-february-linkage-recheck-2026-09-18.md)
tested whether the official February PUF could extend the existing fraud
follow-up. It found zero exact `SCRAMID` overlap with April or June, while the
April–June intersection remains 6,564. Preserve the two-wave boundary and do
not infer a three-wave baseline from the broader 2025 longitudinal design.

The sixteenth rotation deepens the institutional-response arrow through the
[MEPS friction-to-later-panel screen](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-friction-later-panel-v1.md).
It carries route-specific denial or prior-authorization reports on the strict
event/person frame into later same-person health and employment transitions.
The health persistence signal is clearer than the employment pattern, but
neither is verified recovery or remedy; claim timing, appeal, treatment
continuity, household adaptation, trust, action, and exit remain open.

The seventeenth rotation advances the money-and-obligation stage through the
[MEPS event payment and bill-context screen](projects/us-health-cost-household-choice/meps-2024-event-payment-bill-context-v1.md).
It shows that observed self/family payment, total event payment, annual bill
problems, coverage, resources, and care-access reports are separate and often
non-monotonic surfaces. The next test remains a dated obligation with
deductible or balance, payment timing, borrowing or substitution, foregone
care, verified remedy, and recovery.

The eighteenth rotation sharpens the practical-room counterexample through the
[MEPS event payment-band screen](projects/us-health-cost-household-choice/meps-2024-event-payment-bands-v1.md).
Within event families, zero payment can coexist with lower confidence in
covering an unexpected expense, while higher payment can coexist with more
medical debt. These are selection and payer-protection patterns, not payment
effects; the next test remains a same-household obligation and liquidity
episode with timing, substitution, debt, care continuation, remedy, and
recovery.

The nineteenth rotation adds coverage-conditioned event context through the
[MEPS acute-event coverage screen](projects/us-health-cost-household-choice/meps-2024-event-context-by-coverage-v1.md).
It shows that acute-event health and bill-problem surfaces differ across
private, public-only, and uninsured groups, while preserving coverage as a
condition rather than an explanation. The next test remains plan adequacy,
event bill timing, care alternatives, delayed or foregone care, treatment
continuity, remedy, and household outcome.

The twentieth rotation returns to consumer power through [consumer loss and
complaint visibility](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/findings/us-customer-automation-recourse-018.md).
It keeps household fraud exposure, unrecovered money, recovery time, payment
route, complaint routing, and response labels as distinct denominators. The
next test remains a same-customer financial-service case with attempted and
unsubmitted contacts, verified correction or recovery, repeat effort, trust,
switching, and exit.

The twenty-first rotation makes the open middle executable through the [MEPS
staged-ledger stage-coverage audit](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-staged-ledger-stage-coverage-audit-v1.md).
The existing 18,457-row local frame now has a machine-readable coverage report:
trigger and payment/context are observed, choice/route/follow-up are only
reported round proxies, and remedy plus meaning/action are unknown. This is a
depth-control artifact, not a new causal estimate. The next broad pass should
use the same stage vocabulary when advancing another household, consumer,
political, place, institutional, or geopolitical lane.

The twenty-second rotation returns to the cultural and political side of place
through the [migration, local demand, housing, services, and belonging
layer](projects/us-immigration-local-demand/migration-demand-housing-services-belonging-layer-v1.md).
It treats new residents as workers, customers, neighbors, entrepreneurs, and
public-system users simultaneously, while keeping local capacity, unequal
incidence, belonging, attribution, trust, action, and mobility as separate
evidence stages. The existing layer is a cross-source design and not a local
causal estimate; the next pass needs matched place exposure and direct resident
meaning/action measures.

The twenty-third rotation advances the work and firm side through the [firm
capacity, AI adoption, and labor mobility finding](projects/ai-work-control/findings/ai-work-control-064.md).
It keeps establishment training and infrastructure, task-level adoption,
formal representation, and aggregate mobility separate. The next test is a
dated workplace implementation record with monitoring, review, schedule/pay,
grievance, remedy, household, bargaining, and exit outcomes; the present
evidence does not claim worker control or an AI effect.

The twenty-fourth rotation advances housing and place security through the
[place risk, housing security, mobility, and local life layer](projects/us-housing-insurance-affordability/place-risk-mobility-local-life-layer-v1.md).
It keeps rent/mortgage, insurance, hazard, energy, transport, business, and
public-backstop evidence separate while asking who can stay safely, who can
move, and who absorbs the next loss. The next test remains a matched
property/household event with protection, repair, assistance, time/care/work
trade-offs, recovery, and resident meaning; no move or political effect is
claimed from the current place-level surfaces.

The AI/platform lane is refreshed through the [AI use, work, contact, and exit
synthesis](projects/us-digital-habits-attention/ai-use-work-contact-exit-synthesis-v1.md).
It puts ordinary AI adoption, randomized work-time change, synthetic-contact
behavior, companion-product exit tactics, and regulatory inquiry in one
reviewable mechanism map while preserving their separate units and clocks. The
open arrow remains conversion into durable control: data portability, error
correction, captured benefit, worker/consumer alternatives, trust, and later
exit are not jointly observed.

The twenty-fifth rotation advances the financial-access and recourse lane
through [the financial route is part of the social outcome](projects/us-financial-intermediation/financial-access-route-recourse-public-capacity-synthesis-v1.md).
It keeps provider capacity, household account status, nonbank routes, credit
visibility, complaint correction, public fiscal room, household security,
trust, switching, and political judgment separate. The next test remains a
dated account/payment episode with alternatives, terms, effort, response,
remedy, recovery, and exit; the current cross-source layers do not establish
a household financial causal or trust effect.

The twenty-sixth rotation advances the consumer-culture, trust, status, and
everyday-power lane through the [consumer culture, trust, status, and everyday
power layer](projects/us-consumer-culture/consumer-culture-trust-status-layer-v1.md).
It treats buying, searching, sharing data, seeking help, reviewing, reporting,
switching, and staying as distinct participation surfaces that can carry
meaning and generate institutional information. The next test remains a
same-customer dated episode linking need or exposure, social-signaling and
information conditions, cost, response authority, verified remedy, recovery,
switching or abandonment, and later trust or political demand. Participation
is not satisfaction, trust is not consent, and staying is not free choice
without a practical alternative.

The twenty-seventh rotation turns that reader route into a cross-source
proposition through the [consumer participation, trust, and practical power
synthesis](projects/us-consumer-culture/consumer-participation-trust-power-cross-source-synthesis-v1.md).
It places confidence, payment redistribution, platform-mediated civic
attention, charitable-food access, and digital recourse beside one another
without pooling their estimates. The resulting theme is unequal optionality
under institutional mediation: participation can carry money, time, privacy,
attention, safety, stigma, and recovery costs. The next test remains one
dated customer or household episode with alternatives, non-use/non-reporting,
attributed cause, institutional authority, verified remedy, recovery, trust,
and switching or exit.

The twenty-eighth rotation turns the exit question into an observability audit
through the [practical exit observability matrix](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/practical-exit-observability-audit-v1.md).
It places observed SNAP program exits, platform restoration and continued-use
cases, fraud recovery burden, CFPB visibility, insurance-market movement, and
local capacity beside one another without treating any as a common exit rate.
The next test remains a same-unit episode with alternatives, remedy receipt,
switching/non-use, move/stay, and later trust or action.

The first small implementation test is now complete in the [platform-remedy
ledger dry-run](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/practical-exit-platform-ledger-dry-run-v1.md).
Across 27 existing local records, 8 can be labeled `access_restored` from
explicit source language and 19 remain `unknown`; none can be promoted to
practical exit because alternatives, relevant costs or constraints, follow-up,
and protected or sacrificed outcomes are not documented at the same-unit
level. The machine-readable [coverage audit](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/practical-exit-platform-ledger-dry-run-v1.json)
preserves this boundary.

The same contract was then tested against the 25-record CFPB student-loan
event ledger in the [CFPB dry-run](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/cfpb-practical-exit-contract-dry-run-v1.md).
All 25 rows retain observed route/response visibility but `unknown` post-event
status; no same-customer alternative, remedy receipt, recovery, switching,
non-use, trust, or action is established. This sharpens the next collection
requirement without requiring a larger download.

The twenty-ninth rotation adds a second clock to the end-to-end household lane
through the [MEPS event-to-work two-clock audit](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-event-work-two-clock-audit-v1.md).
R4/2 nonemployment levels after strict-window events are aligned by event family
with R5/3 nonemployment, status change, onset, and resolution. Acute-event level
differences persist, but transition directions are mixed and the smaller R5
universe is not row-merged with the R4 screen. This advances the material →
work → later-status arrow while keeping claim identity, alternatives, remedy,
household recovery, meaning, action, and exit open.

The thirtieth rotation audits the retained HTOPS panel record's endpoint
observability through the [local endpoint audit](projects/us-cost-trust-politics/htops-2025-local-endpoint-observability-audit-v1.md).
The record exposes 36 retained measures (32 substantive material/judgment
measures plus four sample/design fields) but
does not represent attribution, distinct civic/political action, remedy,
recovery, switching, or exit. This turns the next acquisition into a precise
dictionary-and-shared-ID check while preserving the distinction between a
missing local artifact and a missing source variable; no large acquisition or
new estimate was made.

The thirty-first rotation extends the local MEPS event spine with the
[prescription-event cascade](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-prescription-event-cascade-v1.md).
Among 18,723 round-order-valid person records, 1,773 had a first prescription
event inside the R3/1-to-R4/2 window. The event-window group has higher later
care-delay, medical-debt, collector-contact, and nonemployment context than
the complementary group. The result advances dated health-to-household
coverage while retaining the missing claim-level need, fill/adherence,
treatment continuity, remedy, recovery, trust, action, and exit arrows; no new
data was downloaded.

The thirty-second rotation carries the prescription-event group to a later
R5/3 clock through the [six-month follow-up](projects/us-household-constraint-cascade/meps-prescription-event-six-month-followup-v1.md).
Of 18,691 round-order-valid people, 724 had an in-window prescription event.
Health worsening is lower in the event-window group, while employment status
change and both nonemployment onset and resolution are higher. The mixed
directions are preserved as selected descriptive context; later status is not
called recovery, and adherence, continuity, remedy, household recovery, trust,
action, and exit remain open.

The thirty-third rotation rechecks the CFPB public complaint schema through a
three-record mortgage probe documented in the [consumer-outcome field audit](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/cfpb-consumer-outcome-field-audit-v1.md).
The current response exposes receipt, routing, response, timeliness, and
place/product fields but no verified correction, repeat effort, account change,
switching, non-use, or post-response assessment; narrative-field visibility
also differs from the earlier probe. This updates the schema boundary without
turning response labels into remedy or exit evidence, and no bulk data was
acquired.


The consumer-power lane was refreshed on 2026-09-13 with a live CFPB
aggregate-only API snapshot and reusable fetcher. This is current
institutional-response evidence, not a verified consumer-outcome result.
The annual 2020–2024 comparison now adds time while retaining taxonomy,
routing, and publication-rule breaks as explicit counterinterpretations.
The [case-route sample](projects/us-customer-automation-recourse/cfpb-case-route-sample-2024-v1.md)
adds a bounded case-level receipt-to-company-routing diagnostic, with
product-conditioned visibility and response labels. It does not estimate
population route rates or verified remedy; same-case effort, outcome, trust,
switching, and exit remain open.

The Bilt/CFPB update adds a distinct collaborative-implementation stage to
the consumer remedy lane: proactive outreach, a fee-reimbursement process,
review of more than 500 newly identified customers, and continued Bureau
monitoring are visible, while person-level payment, account correction,
adequacy, later security, trust, switching, and exit remain open. The CFPB
Ombudsman examples of address changes, reissued checks, and third-party
administrator problems preserve the reachability counterpoint. This advances
remedy-stage coverage without claiming completed recovery; the next decisive
test remains a same-customer follow-up with verified receipt and later outcome.

The household fraud lane now includes a codebook-backed SHED subgroup layer
conditioning exposure, unrecovered money, and recovery time on age, income, and
account payment route. It strengthens the household loss-to-burden stage while
leaving verified firm remedy, digital-access and general disability gradients,
later trust, switching, and exit open.

The platform/data/attention lane now includes the [Pew 2026 AI daily-life,
control, and public-concern layer](projects/us-digital-habits-attention/pew-2026-ai-daily-life-control-layer-v1.md).
It adds current U.S. adult measures of chatbot adoption, frequency, purpose,
age differences, perceived usefulness, privacy concern, pace, and confidence in
government regulation. It does not establish dependence, harm, actual privacy
loss, product causation, or practical ability to leave.

The same lane now includes a separate [Pew 2025 adult social-media-use
layer](projects/us-digital-habits-attention/pew-adult-social-media-use-2025-layer-v1.md)
and [bounded finding](projects/us-digital-habits-attention/findings/us-digital-habits-attention-004.md).
It provides adult platform reach, repeated-wave change, frequency, and
age/gender/race-and-ethnicity patterning. The adult sample is kept separate
from the teen platform/chatbot survey and from the frequency sample; reach is
not treated as logged attention, content exposure, persuasion, or exit cost.

The [FTC AI-companion inquiry record](records/us-ftc-ai-companion-inquiry-2025.json)
adds a separate institutional response: seven companies received information
requests about monetization, data use, safety testing, age limits, disclosures,
and possible effects on children and teens. The inquiry is not treated as
evidence of harm or enforcement outcome.

The [World Bank WDR 2026 AI capability and governance record](records/us-world-bank-wdr2026-ai-capability-governance.json)
separates the adopt/adapt/advance framework, value-chain concentration, local
complements, public-service evaluation, and political-power analysis from US
outcome estimates. Its detailed [source-specific layer](projects/ai-work-control/world-bank-wdr2026-ai-capability-governance-layer-v1.md)
and [project finding](projects/ai-work-control/findings/ai-work-control-017.md)
preserve the report's comparative and policy-analysis boundaries.
